<DESCRIPTION>TESLA MOTORS, INC. THIRD QUARTER 2011 SHAREHOLDER LETTER
<TEXT>
<HTML><HEAD>
<TITLE>Tesla Motors, Inc. Third Quarter 2011 Shareholder Letter</TITLE>
</HEAD>
 <BODY BGCOLOR="WHITE">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="right"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Exhibit 99.1 </B></FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" WIDTH="100%" BORDER="0" STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" ALIGN="center">


<TR>
<TD WIDTH="10%"></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom" WIDTH="2%"></TD>
<TD WIDTH="88%"></TD></TR>
<TR>
<TD VALIGN="bottom" NOWRAP> <P STYLE="border-bottom:1px solid #000000;width:57pt">


<IMG SRC="g249698g17m45.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
</P></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom"> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Tesla Motors, Inc. &#150; Third Quarter 2011 Shareholder Letter</B></FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" align="left">&nbsp;</P> <P><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Start of Model S deliveries on track for no later than July 2012</B></FONT><br><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Model S customer reservations continue accelerating</B></FONT><br><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Supplier partnerships
in place to achieve gross margin of 25% in 2013</B></FONT><br><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Total cash position improved from last quarter</B></FONT><br><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;LOI from Daimler for a full powertrain for an electric Mercedes</B></FONT><br><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Multiple
agreements signed for sale of Model S ZEV/GHG credits</B></FONT><br><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>&#149;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Full year revenue guidance raised to between $195M and $200M</B></FONT></P></TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">November&nbsp;2, 2011 </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Dear Fellow
Shareholders, </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We are pleased to report another quarter of strong performance. Total revenues in the third quarter were $58
million, up 85% from Q3 of last year. Automotive sales grew by almost 11% from last quarter due to solid Roadster demand and powertrain component sales. Total gross margin was 30%, our fifth consecutive quarter of achieving 30% or higher gross
margin. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%;padding-bottom:0px;"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The highlight of the quarter was our Model S Beta reveal. We opened the doors of our Fremont
factory during the weekend of October&nbsp;1</FONT><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="1"><SUP STYLE="vertical-align:baseline; position:relative; bottom:.8ex">st</SUP></FONT><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"> to
thousands of reservation holders, media and investors. Participants rode in Model S Beta vehicles and toured the Tesla Factory. They witnessed firsthand the progress we have made in developing the Model S and our manufacturing facility. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The Model S development program remains on schedule for first customer deliveries of the Model S no later than July 2012. In addition, we
continue to have confidence in our long term target gross margin of 25% for Model S upon realizing the manufacturing efficiencies associated with an annualized sales run rate of 20,000 units. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Our non-GAAP net loss of $0.55 per share this quarter, or $0.63 per share on a GAAP basis, reflects our continued investments in R&amp;D
and corporate infrastructure to support the launch of Model S.&nbsp;As we have discussed since our IPO, net losses will obviously continue until we reach volume sales of the Model S in 2013. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We are excited about the many bargains available for used tooling and equipment, some of which are located quite near to our Fremont
factory. We believe in purchasing used tooling&nbsp;and equipment for pennies on the dollar and buying low cost desks from IKEA, rather than spending large sums of money on architectural and interior design. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Strong Roadster Demand&nbsp;&amp; Margin </U></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" WIDTH="100%" BORDER="0" STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" ALIGN="center">

<TD VALIGN="bottom" WIDTH="1%"></TD>
<TD WIDTH="48%"></TD></TR>


<TR>
<TD VALIGN="top"> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:2.00em"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We continue to experience strong demand for the Roadster. During the quarter, we delivered 184 Roadsters, up 22%
from the same quarter last year. We sold more Roadsters in the United States than we have in any quarter over the past two years. Total deliveries worldwide now exceed 2,000.</FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:1px; margin-left:2.00em"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We also generated over $28 million in Roadster-related revenue
in this quarter, up 56% from the same quarter last year, and slightly higher than</FONT></P></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD VALIGN="top" ALIGN="center">


<IMG SRC="g249698image002.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
</TD></TR>
</TABLE>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">
the prior quarter, due to higher volume, higher average selling prices, slightly lower lease mix and solid service revenue. Automotive gross margin rose to 24%, primarily due to higher average
selling prices, additional service revenue and ongoing cost control on the Roadster. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The only slightly negative aspect of
Roadster sales was ongoing disproportionately lower sales in the UK due to the continuing adverse impact on the Roadster via reruns of &#147;Top Gear&#148;, the UK&#146;s leading car show. As a result, we had an excess inventory of right hand drive
versions of the Roadster and continue to incur additional costs to correct the consumer misperception. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Our Roadster owners
have now driven their cars 16&nbsp;million electrically powered miles, the equivalent of traveling to the moon and back over 30 times. As the world&#146;s longest running electric vehicle program, the Roadster has helped us understand real world
consumer behavior related to electric vehicles, service requirements and overall vehicle performance. This experience will pay great dividends when we bring the Model S to market. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Showcasing Model S Development and Factory Preparation </U></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" WIDTH="100%" BORDER="0" STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" ALIGN="center">

<TD VALIGN="top"> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:2.00em"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">During the weekend of October&nbsp;1</FONT><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="1"><SUP
STYLE="vertical-align:baseline; position:relative; bottom:.8ex">st</SUP></FONT><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">, we welcomed almost 3,000 Model S reservation holders, journalists and investors to our Tesla Factory.</FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:1px; margin-left:2.00em"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The highlight of the event was the test rides in the Model S
Beta vehicles. Guests were able to experience the acceleration and handling of the car, and see the features of the 17-inch touchscreen. The Model S also showcased the two rear facing child seats and plenty of storage space under the hood. The
response has been positive&#151;including from the automotive press. Please check out the review from MotorTrend, in particular.</FONT></P></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD VALIGN="top" ALIGN="center">


<IMG SRC="g249698image003.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
</TD></TR>
</TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We announced two significant improvements to the Model S at the event. First, we intend to make a
performance version of the Model S available at the start of production. We anticipate that this option will enable the Model S to accelerate from 0 to 60 in about 4.4 seconds, faster than a standard Porsche 911 Carrera! Second, we announced an
aerodynamic wheel option that is intended to extend the range of the Model S up to almost 320 miles. This range is almost four times more than any other fully electric vehicle on the market (except, of course, the Tesla Roadster). We also released a
more detailed set of Model S features. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>

factory equipment and robots needed to manufacture the Model S have now been installed. This includes installations in the stamping, plastics, paint, body and final assembly shops, which were operational in dry-cycle mode. Customers were also
able</FONT></P></TD></TR>

conveyors.</FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:1px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The public display of the Model S Beta vehicles was tangible
evidence that our Beta build continues on schedule. During the Beta phase, cars will be produced using increasing maturity of production parts and manufacturing processes. We plan to</FONT></P></TD></TR>
</TABLE>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">
produce about 50 Beta vehicles in this phase for more testing, validation, systems integration and marketing efforts. After the Beta phase, we plan to begin building Release Candidate cars during
the first half of 2012. During that phase of the program, we intend to fine-tune production and assembly processes to build high quality cars for sales to our customers. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Our confidence in the progress of both the Model S Beta phase and the Tesla Factory permits us to be more specific in our timing guidance for first customer deliveries. We are on plan to deliver the first
Model S vehicles to customers no later than July 2012. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We also recently finalized a long term supply contract with Panasonic
for cells used in the Model S, which gives us increased visibility into our variable costs. As a result, we continue to have confidence in our long term target gross margin of 25% for Model S upon realizing the manufacturing efficiencies associated
with an annualized sales run rate of 20,000 units. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:18px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>

<TD VALIGN="top"> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Retail Footprint&nbsp;&amp; Reservations Growing</U></B></FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:1px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We are continuing to expand our company-owned retail<BR>network. We opened a new
store in Eindhoven, Netherlands<BR>during the quarter and just recently unveiled a gallery in<BR>Houston, Texas. We now have 20 locations globally. Additional<BR>stores utilizing the new store concept will open during the fourth<BR>quarter in
Bellevue, Washington, Chicago, Illinois and Newport<BR>Beach, California. Our existing locations in some of these<BR>markets will be converted into service centers in preparation for<BR>the Model S launch.</FONT></P></TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Our goals with the new store concept are to engage, excite and inform future electric vehicle buyers. The response to this new strategy
continues to surpass our expectations &#151; over 170,000 people have visited our two new stores in San Jose, California and Denver, Colorado during the third quarter alone. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center">


<IMG SRC="g249698image004a.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
 </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Reservations growth for Model S continued to accelerate, as we added about 1,150 net new
reservations during the quarter. This brings our total count to about 6,500 reservations as of the end of September. It is likely that the anticipation of the Model S Beta reveal stimulated an increase in reservations; however, we are continuing to
see strong ongoing interest as we begin to display the Model S at some of our stores. These reservations require a minimum $5,000, refundable deposit. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Model X Advanced Engineering on Plan </U></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We are excited about how the
design and functionality of the Model X is beginning to mature. Our vision for the Model X is a vehicle that combines the functionality of a minivan with a design as cool as an SUV. We remain on plan for an exclusive showing later this year.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Since we are leveraging the Model S architecture and powertrain for Model X, we continue to anticipate that Model X can be
brought to production quickly for first customer deliveries in late 2013, with volumes ramping to 10,000&#151;15,000 units per year beginning in 2014, and at prices comparable to Model S. Importantly, much of the equipment we are currently
installing at the Tesla Factory for the Model S will also be used for the Model X. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Solid Powertrain Execution </U></B></FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>

<TD VALIGN="top"> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We continued to execute well on our existing powertrain contracts<BR>during the third quarter. Our production agreements with
Daimler<BR>and our development services agreement with Toyota produced<BR>combined revenue of over $29 million in the period. Powertrain<BR>component related sales accounted for almost $15 million this<BR>quarter, a new quarterly revenue record. We
still expect to<BR>complete all deliveries under the Smart fortwo and A-Class<BR>programs on schedule by the end of this year.</FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:1px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We continue to make good progress on the development of the<BR>RAV4 EV with Toyota. Once again, we met all of the milestones<BR>planned for the quarter in
the development contract for the RAV4<BR>EV&#146;s full powertrain system. This drove solid development services<BR>revenue of over $14 million for the third quarter.</FONT></P></TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Overall, the RAV4 EV development program remains on schedule and should be completed by early 2012, as planned. Thereafter, we plan to
begin shipping complete powertrain systems for the RAV4 EV to Toyota, under a multi-year contract of approximately $100 million. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We are also pleased to announce the receipt of a letter of intent from Daimler for a full powertrain program for a vehicle in the Mercedes line. Details of this deal remain confidential, but will be
announced in the coming months. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Strong Financial Performance&nbsp;&amp; Improved Outlook </U></B></FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Given the strong demand we continue to see for Tesla Roadsters and solid execution in our powertrain activities, we now project that full
year revenues will be between $195 and $200 million, up from our prior guidance of $180 to $190 million. Total gross margin this quarter was 30%, down only slightly from Q2 due to lower mix and margin contribution from development services revenue.
As we have mentioned in the past, timing differences between </FONT></P>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">
recognition of revenue and the underlying costs of development services can cause the gross margin of our development contracts to vary from quarter to quarter. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Our operating expenses continue to reflect our investments in new vehicle development and the build-out of our infrastructure. Our total
operating expenses were $82 million on a GAAP basis and $74 million on a non-GAAP basis, which excludes stock-based compensation expense. A full reconciliation between the non-GAAP and GAAP information is included at the end of this Shareholder
Letter. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;
</P><DIV STYLE="position:relative;float:left; margin-right:1%; width:48%;padding-right:9px;padding-bottom:8px;overflow:hidden;padding-top:3px">
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:29px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Almost two-thirds of our operating expenses relate to our investments in research and
development. R&amp;D spending increased very slightly from last quarter, reflecting ongoing costs of developing the Model&nbsp;S and increased activity in preparing the Tesla Factory. Since pre-production costs are expensed under GAAP, the
development activities on the Model&nbsp;S and Model&nbsp;X drive our net losses. Sales of Model&nbsp;S will enable us to grow our revenue very substantially starting next year and set the foundation for further growth in future years. Consequently,
these continuing pre-production losses in the short term reflect our strategic decision to ramp up our business to the next level.
</FONT></P></DIV><DIV STYLE="position:relative;float:right; width:48%;padding-right:9px;padding-bottom:8px;overflow:hidden;padding-top:3px">
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:27px">


<IMG SRC="g249698image008.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
 </P></DIV><div style="clear:both; height:0px; font-size:0px">&nbsp;</div>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Selling, general and administrative expenses also rose from last quarter driven by higher
store-related and marketing activities, including the opening of new stores and hiring of support staff. We expect our operating expenses to rise slightly as we continue to prepare the Tesla Factory for production, refine the Model S, continue the
advanced engineering work on Model X, and add more stores. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Capital expenditures were about $69 million this quarter as we
continued to build out the Tesla Factory and invest in tooling for Model S. We now expect our capital expenditures for 2011 to be at the lower end of our previous guidance of between $220 and $245 million. This is despite continued controlled
expenditure on certain projects that we believe best serve the company by remaining confidential at this time. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">In total, our
non-GAAP net loss for the quarter was almost $57 million, or $0.55 per share, reflecting 104.1&nbsp;million weighted common shares outstanding. The increase from 97.8&nbsp;million weighted common shares outstanding last quarter reflects an entire
quarter of the incremental 8.1&nbsp;million shares issued in our follow-on offering and concurrent private placements in June. We therefore expect that our share count will remain relatively stable at this level. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">We will provide guidance for 2012 in our Shareholder Letter for Q4 2011 results. This outlook will include some commentary about the
first and second halves of 2012, given the impact of the start of deliveries of Model S in the middle of the year. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><U>Sufficient Capital
Resources for Model S and Model X </U></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">At the end of the third quarter we had cash resources on our balance sheet of $334
million, higher than last quarter&#146;s balance of $331 million. During the quarter, we drew down almost $91 million from our DoE loan facility. Our projects continue to be on track with the DoE per the terms of the loan agreement, and our
relationship with the DoE remains strong. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Combining our $334 million of cash on hand, with the additional $240 million we
have left to draw on our loan facility with the DoE, we now have approximately $574 million in available capital resources. We believe that these funds will be sufficient to develop and deliver Model S and Model X, based on our current plans.
</FONT></P>

Model X; the ability to achieve revenue, gross margin and spending targets; the ability of Tesla to produce vehicles at the Tesla Factory in Fremont, California as well as the plans and schedule for the Tesla Factory and the equipment there; the
schedules related to, and the financial results expected from,Tesla&#146;s development programs with Daimler and Toyota; our ability to execute multiple product development programs simultaneously; the sufficiency of current available funds to

statements are based on management&#146;s current expectations, and as a result of certain risks and uncertainties actual results may differ materially from those projected. The following important factors, without limitation, could cause actual
results to differ materially from those in the forward-looking statements: Tesla&#146;s future success depends on its ability to design and achieve market acceptance of new vehicle models, specifically Model S and Model X; the risk of delays in the
design, manufacture, launch and financing of Model S, including the build-out of its planned Model S manufacturing facility; the risk of a decline in revenues prior to the launch of Model S; consumers&#146; willingness to adopt electric vehicles and
electric cars in particular; Tesla&#146;s ability to fully draw down on its facility from the U.S. Department of Energy; risks associated with the ability to achieve the expected financial results from the production of powertrain systems for the
Toyota RAV4 EV; risks associated with sales and anticipated delivery schedule of the Tesla Roadster; competition in the automotive market generally and the alternative fuel vehicle market in particular; Tesla&#146;s ability to establish, maintain
and strengthen the Tesla brand; the unavailability, reduction or elimination of governmental and economic incentives for electric vehicles; Tesla&#146;s ability to establish, maintain and strengthen its relationships with strategic partners such as
Daimler, Toyota and Panasonic; and Tesla&#146;s ability to execute on its plans for its new interactive retail strategy and for new store openings. More information on potential factors that could affect the Company&#146;s financial results is
included from time to time in Tesla&#146;s Securities and Exchange Commission filings and reports, including the risks identified under the section captioned &#147;Risk Factors&#148; in its quarterly report on Form 10-Q filed on August&nbsp;12, 2011
and its registration statement on Form S-1 filed on June&nbsp;2, 2011. Tesla disclaims any obligation to update information contained in these forward-looking statements whether as a result of new information, future events, or otherwise.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Non-GAAP Financial Information </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Consolidated financial information has been presented in accordance with GAAP as well as on a non-GAAP basis.&nbsp;On a non-GAAP basis, financial measures exclude non-cash items such as stock-based
compensation as well as the change in fair value related to Tesla&#146;s warrant liability.&nbsp;Management believes that it is useful to supplement its GAAP financial statements with this non-GAAP information because management uses such
information internally for its operating, budgeting and financial planning purposes.&nbsp;These non-GAAP financial measures also facilitate management&#146;s internal comparisons to Tesla&#146;s historical performance as well as comparisons to the
operating results of other companies.&nbsp;In addition, Tesla believes these non-GAAP financial measures are useful to investors because they allow for greater transparency into the indicators used by management as a basis for its financial and
operational decision making.&nbsp;Non-GAAP information is not prepared under a comprehensive set of accounting rules and therefore, should only be read in conjunction with financial information reported under U.S. GAAP when understanding
Tesla&#146;s operating performance.&nbsp;A reconciliation between GAAP and non-GAAP financial information is provided below. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:12px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>

of other companies. Non-GAAP information is not prepared under a comprehensive set of accounting rules and therefore, should only be read in conjunction with financial information reported under U.S. GAAP w hen understanding Tesla&#146;s operating
performance. A reconciliation between GAAP and non-GAAP financial information is provided above. </FONT></P>
